LLaMA-3.3-70B-FP8 在 98GB GPU 上应该能 fit（FP8 ~70GB）\n   - `max_model_len` 可能需要调整（LLaMA 3.3 context 131072，但我们的 prompt 不超过 4096）\n   - 检查 vLLM 0.20.2 是否支持 LLaMA 3.3 FP8（nvidia 格式）\n\n4. **如果需要代码修改**: \n   - # 项目: schema-dag-extraction

## 运行环境

## 任务：准备 exp-012 multi-model LLaMA-3.3-70B 实验代码

### 背景
exp-012 用 LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct-FP8 复制 Few-NERD exp1+exp2，验证 multi-model generalizability（Claim 1 需 >=2 models）。

### 服务器
- cqa1-38874
- conda base: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- 项目路径: /root/schema-dag-extraction
- LLaMA 模型路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8/`（可能还在下载中，先检查）
- **GPU 被 exp-008 占用（PID 241208），不要启动 GPU 任务**

### 步骤

1. **检查 LLaMA 下载状态**:
   ```bash
   ls /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8/*.safetensors 2>/dev/null | wc -l
   ls /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8/config.json 2>/dev/null
   # 需要 15 个 safetensors 分片 + config.json + tokenizer 文件
   ```

2. **检查代码兼容性**: 读 `src/run_experiment.py` 和 `src/run_extraction.py`，确认：
   - `--model_path` 参数是否正确传递到 vLLM
   - LLaMA-3.3-70B-FP8 是否需要特殊 vLLM 参数（如 `--max-model-len`、`--tensor-parallel-size`、`--quantization`）
   - LLaMA 3.3 的 chat template 是否与 Qwen3 不同（vLLM 通常自动检测）
   - prompt 格式是否需要调整（system/user 角色标记差异）

3. **关键适配点**：
   - LLaMA-3.3-70B-FP8 在 98GB GPU 上应该能 fit（FP8 ~70GB）
   - `max_model_len` 可能需要调整（LLaMA 3.3 context 131072，但我们的 prompt 不超过 4096）
   - 检查 vLLM 0.20.2 是否支持 LLaMA 3.3 FP8（nvidia 格式）

4. **如果需要代码修改**: 
   - 在 `src/run_extraction.py` 中确保 LLaMA chat template 正确应用
   - 如有必要加 `--model_family llama` 参数
   - 不要改动影响 Qwen3 实验的代码路径

5. **Dry-run（不用 GPU）**:
   - `python3 src/run_experiment.py --help` 确认参数
   - 检查是否有 hardcoded 的 Qwen3-specific 逻辑

6. **检查 Qwen3 BCS embeddings 是否可复用**:
   - exp-012 用 LLaMA 做 extraction，但 BCS 仍用 Qwen3 embeddings？还是应该用 LLaMA embeddings？
   - 根据 D010 发现（extraction model alignment matters），建议也计算 LLaMA embeddings for Few-NERD。但这需要 GPU。先记录这个 decision point。

### 产出
- 代码修改（如需要）
- 兼容性报告：LLaMA 是否可直接用现有脚本跑，还是需要适配
- LLaMA 下载状态确认
- exp-012 launch 命令草案

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Compute type embeddings using a causal LM (e.g. Qwen3).\\n\\nInput: Type definitions JSON, pretrained model\\nOutput: {type_name: embedding_tensor} saved as .pt\\nDependencies: torch, transformers\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport logging\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\ndef compute_embeddings(\\n    model_path: str,\\n    type_definitions: dict,\\n    device: str = \\\"cuda:0\\\",\\n    batch_size: int = 8,\\n) -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute mean-pooled last-layer hidden states for each type.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        model_path: Path to pretrained causal LM.\\n        type_definitions: {type_name: definition_text}.\\n        device: Torch device string.\\n        batch_size: Number of types to process at once.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Dict mapping type_name -> embedding tensor (1D, hidden_dim).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading model from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n        device_map=device,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n    )\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    type_names = list(type_definitions.keys())\\n    type_texts = [\\n        f\\\"Entity type: {name}. Definition: {defn}.\\\"\\n        for name, defn in type_definitions.items()\\n    ]\\n\\n    embeddings = {}\\n    for i in range(0, len(type_texts), batch_size):\\n        batch_names = type_names[i : i + batch_size]\\n        batch_texts = type_texts[i : i + batch_size]\\n\\n        inputs = tokenizer(\\n            batch_texts, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", padding=True, truncation=True, max_length=512\\n        ).to(device)\\n\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = model(**inputs, output_hidden_states=True)\\n\\n        last_hidden = outputs.hidden_states[-1]  # [B, seq_len, hidden_dim]\\n        attention_mask = inputs[\\\"attention_mask\\\"].unsqueeze(-1)  # [B, seq_len, 1]\\n        masked_hidden = last_hidden * attention_mask\\n        summed = masked_hidden.sum(dim=1)  # [B, hidden_dim]\\n        counts = attention_mask.sum(dim=1)  # [B, 1]\\n        mean_pooled = (summed / counts).cpu()  # [B, hidden_dim]\\n\\n        for j, name in enumerate(batch_names):\\n            embeddings[name] = mean_pooled[j]\\n\\n        logger.info(\\\"Computed embeddings for types %d-%d / %d\\\", i, i + len(batch_names), len(type_names))\\n\\n    return embeddings\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Compute type embeddings with a causal LM\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model_path\\\", required=True, help=\\\"Path to pretrained model\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--types_file\\\", required=True, help=\\\"Type definitions JSON path\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output_path\\\", required=True, help=\\\"Output .pt file path\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--device\\\", default=\\\"cuda:0\\\", help=\\\"Torch device\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=int, default=8, help=\\\"Batch size for encoding\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\n\\n    import json\\n    with open(args.types_file, \\\"r\\\") as f:\\n        type_definitions = json.load(f)\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loaded %d type definitions\\\", len(type_definitions))\\n    embeddings = compute_embeddings(args.model_path, type_definitions, args.device, args.batch_size)\\n\\n    Path(args.output_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    torch.save(embeddings, args.output_path)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saved embeddings to %s\\\", args.output_path)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.53}}"}

      type_desc_lines.append(f\\\"- {t}: {defn}\\\")\\n    type_desc = \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(type_desc_lines)\\n\\n    return (\\n        f\\\"Extract all named entities from the following text. \\\"\\n batches_to_run.append((\\\"high_bcs\\\", strategies[\\\"high_bcs\\\"][\\\"types\\\"], strategies[\\\"high_bcs\\\"][\\\"bcs\\\"]))\\n\\n        # Random batches\\n        if \\\"random\\\" in strategies:\\n            for ri, rb in enumerate(strategies[\\\"random\\\"]):\\n                batches_to_run.append((f\\\"random_{ri}\\\", rb[\\\"types\\\"], rb[\\\"bcs\\\"]))\\n\\n        for batch_id, batch_types, batch_bcs in batches_to_run:\\n            full_batch_id = f\\\"{count_key}_{batch_id}\\\"\\n            type_set = set(batch_types)\\n            logger.info(\\\"Running batch %s (%d types, BCS=%.4f) on %d samples\\\",\\n                        full_batch_id, len(batch_types), batch_bcs, len(samples))\\n\\n            # Per-batch JSON schema with type enum constraint\\n \\\"end\\\"],\\n        },\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt_v1(input_text: str, type_definitions: Dict[str, str], type_subset: List[str]) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build NER extraction prompt             \\\"text\\\": {\\\"type\\\": \\\"string\\\"},\\n                \\\"type\\\": {\\\"type\\\": \\\"string\\\", \\\"enum\\\": sorted(batch_types)},\\n            },\\n            \\\"required\\\": [\\\"text\\\", \\\"type\\\"],\\n        },\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt_v2(input_text: str, type_definitions: Dict[str, str], type_subset: List[str]) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build NER extraction prompt without offsets (v2: text+type only).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    type_desc_lines = []\\n    for t in sorted(type_subset):\\n        defn = type_definitions.get(t, t)\\n        type_desc_lines.append(f\\\"- {t}: {defn}\\\")\\n    type_desc = \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(type_desc_lines)\\n\\n    return (\\n        f\\\"Extract all named entities from the following text. \\\"\\n        f\\\"Only extract entities of the following types:\\\\n{type_desc}\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n        f\\\"Text: {input_text}\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n        f\\\"Only extract entities that clearly match one of the given types. \\\"\\n        f\\\"If no entities of these types exist in the text, output an empty list [].\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n        f'Output as JSON: [{{\\\"text\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}}]'\\n    )\\n\\n\\n# Backward-compatible aliases\\nbuild_json_schema = build_json_schema_v1\\nbuild_prompt = build_prompt_v1\\n\\n# --- Event Detection prompts (for MAVEN-style tasks) ---\\n\\ndef build_prompt_v2_event(input_text: str, type_definitions: dict, type_subset: list) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build event trigger extraction prompt (v2: text+type only).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    type_desc_lines = []\\n    for t in sorted(type_subset):\\n        defn = type_definitions.get(t, t)\\n        type_desc_lines.append(f\\\"- {t}: {defn}\\\")\\n    type_desc = \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(type_desc_lines)\\n\\n    return (\\n        f\\\"Extract all event triggers from the following text. \\\"\\n        f\\\"A trigger is the word or phrase that most clearly expresses triggers that clearly match one of the given event types. \\\"\\n        f\\\"If no events of these types exist in the text, output an empty list [].\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n        f'Output as JSON:    },\\n        \\\"required\\\": [\\\"relation\\\"],\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt_v2_re(sentence: str, head: str, tail: str,\\n                       relation_defs: Dict[str, str],\\n                       relation_types: List[str]) -> str:\\n    candidates = \\\", \\\".join(sorted(relation_types))\\n    return (\\n        \\\"Given the following sentence and two entities, \\\"\\n        \\\"classify the relation between them.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n        f'Sentence: \\\"{sentence}\\\"\\\\n'\\n        f'Head entity: \\\"{head}\\\"\\\\n'\\n        f'Tail entity: \\\"{tail}\\\"\\\\n\\\\n'\\n        f\\\"Candidate relations: {candidates}\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n        'Output a single JSON object: {\\\"relation\\\": \\\"&lt;relation_type>\\\"}\\\\n'\\n        'If none of the candidate relations apply, output: {\\\"relation\\\": \\\"none\\\"}'\\n    )\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\ndef run_extraction(\\n    model_path: str,\\n    samples: List[Dict],\\n    batch_config: Dict,\\n    type_definitions: vllm.sampling_params import StructuredOutputsParams\\n\\n    if task == \\\"re\\\":\\n        schema_fn = build_json_schema_re\\n        prompt_fn = None  # RE uses build_prompt_v2_re with different signature\\n    elif prompt_version == \\\"v2\\\":\\n        schema_fn = build_json_schema_v2\\n        prompt_fn = build_prompt_v2_event if task == \\\"event_detection\\\" else build_prompt_v2\\n    else:\\n        schema_fn = build_json_schema_v1\\n        prompt_fn = build_prompt_v1_event if task == \\\"event_detection\\\" else build_prompt_v1\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading vLLM model from %s (prompt_version=%s)\\\", model_path, prompt_version)\\n    llm = LLM(\\n        model=model_path,\\n        dtype=\\\"bfloat16\\\",\\n        gpu_memory_utilization=0.95,\\n        enforce_eager=True,\\n        max_model_len=max_model_len,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        disable_log_stats=True,\\n    )\\n\\n    all_results = []\\n\\n    for count_key, strategies in batch_config.items():\\n        batches_to_run = []\\n\\n        # Low BCS batch\\n        if \\\"low_bcs\\\" in strategies:\\n            batches_to_run.append((\\\"low_bcs\\\", strategies[\\\"low_bcs\\\"][\\\"types\\\"], strategies[\\\"low_bcs\\\"][\\\"bcs\\\"]))\\n\\n        # High BCS batch\\n        if \\\"high_bcs\\\" in strategies:\\n llm = LLM(
    model=model_path,
    dtype="bfloat16",
    gpu_memory_utilization=0.95,
    enforce_eager=True,
    max_model_len=max_model_len,
    trust_remote_code=True,
    disable_log_stats=True,
)
```

Key observations:
1. `dtype="bfloat16"` — but LLaMA-3.3-70B-FP8 is already FP8 quantized (nvidia modelopt format). 

vLLM should auto-detect the FP8 quantization from `hf_quant_config.json` and apply it automatically, with `bfloat16` serving as the compute dtype for non-quantized layers, so that should work fine.

2. No `tensor_parallel_size` specified — defaults to 1. LLaMA-3.3-70B-FP8 is roughly 70GB, and with an A800's 80GB VRAM and `gpu_memory_utilization=0.95` giving ~76GB usable, fitting the model weights plus KV cache is going to be tight. Though the model has 15 safetensors shards and similar FP8 models run on this setup, so it might work — I should check the actual GPU configuration and what "cqa1-38874" indicates about the hardware.

Actually, the task specifies 98GB GPU, which is likely an A100 80GB or larger, so a 70GB FP8 model should fit comfortably with room for KV cache. With `tensor_parallel_size` defaulting to 1, this should be fine.

The prompts are plain text strings passed directly to `llm.generate()` without chat formatting, so vLLM will tokenize them as raw text without applying a chat template — which is actually what we want here since the prompts are already designed for that.

The code is completely model-agnostic with no model-specific conditionals, so swapping in LLaMA-3.3-70B-FP8 is just a matter of changing the `--model_path` flag. The `dtype="bfloat16"` parameter will work fine since vLLM auto-detects FP8 from the quantization config, and memory-wise we're looking at roughly 70GB for the model plus KV cache on a 98GB GPU, which should fit within the available headroom.

I need to resume the LLaMA download since two shards are missing, and then I can compute the embeddings using the same model-agnostic embedding script by pointing it to the LLaMA model path.

The key decision for experiment 012 is whether to use Qwen3 embeddings (which are ready now but won't capture LLaMA's actual type confusion patterns) or compute LLaMA embeddings (which requires GPU access currently tied up by experiment 008, but would give BCS values that truly reflect LLaMA's confusion space).

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Few-NERD data loading and preprocessing utilities.\\n\\nInput: Few-NERD dataset (token-level annotation, 66 fine-grained entity types)\\n       Loaded from local parquet files; falls back to HF Hub if parquet missing.\\nOutput: List of {\\\"text\\\": str, \\\"entities\\\": [{\\\"text\\\": str, \\\"type\\\": str, \\\"start\\\": int, \\\"end\\\": int}]}\\nDependencies: datasets\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set\\n\\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nDEFAULT_DATA_DIR = \\\"/root/schema-dag-extraction/data/parquet\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_few_nerd(\\n    split: str = \\\"test\\\",\\n    max_samples: Optional[int] = None,\\n    data_dir: str = DEFAULT_DATA_DIR,\\n) -> List[Dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load Few-NERD and convert token-level tags to span-level entities.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        split: Dataset split (\\\"train\\\", \\\"validation\\\", \\\"test\\\").\\n        max_samples: If set, only return first N samples.\\n        data_dir: Directory containing {split}.parquet files.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List of dicts with \\\"text\\\" (reconstructed sentence) and \\\"entities\\\" (span-level).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    parquet_path = os.path.join(data_dir, f\\\"{split}.parquet\\\")\\n    if os.path.exists(parquet_path):\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading Few-NERD from local parquet: %s\\\", parquet_path)\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"parquet\\\", data_files=parquet_path, split=\\\"train\\\")\\n    else:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Local parquet not found, falling back to HF Hub\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"DFKI-SLT/few-nerd\\\", \\\"supervised\\\", split=split, trust_remote_code=True)\\n\\n    tag_names = ds.features[\\\"fine_ner_tags\\\"].feature.names\\n\\n    samples = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(ds):\\n        if max_samples is not None and i >= max_samples:\\n            break\\n\\n        tokens = example[\\\"tokens\\\"]\\n        tags = example[\\\"fine_ner_tags\\\"]\\n\\n        text, entities = _tokens_to_spans(tokens, tags, tag_names)\\n        samples.append({\\\"text\\\": text, \\\"entities\\\": entities})\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loaded %d samples from Few-NERD %s split\\\", len(samples), split)\\n    return samples\\n\\n\\ndef _tokens_to_spans(tokens: List[str], tag_ids: List[int], tag_names: List[str]) -> tuple:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert token-level tags to char-offset spans.\\n\\n    Few-NERD uses flat labels (no B-/I- prefix). Consecutive tokens sharing\\n    the same non-O tag id form one entity span.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (text, entities) where text is space-joined tokens and entities have char offsets.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    text = \\\"\\\"\\n    char_offsets = []\\n    for tok in tokens:\\n        start = len(text)\\n        text += tok\\n        end = len(text)\\n        char_offsets.append((start, end))\\n        text += \\\" \\\"\\n    text = text.rstrip()\\n\\n    entities = []\\n    current_entity = None\\n\\n    for idx, tag_id in enumerate(tag_ids):\\n        if tag_id == 0:  # O tag\\n            if current_entity is not None:\\n                entities.append(_finalize_entity(current_entity, tokens, char_offsets))\\n                current_entity = None\\n        else:\\n            type_name = tag_names[tag_id]\\n            if current_entity is not None and current_entity[\\\"type\\\"] == type_name:\\n                current_entity[\\\"token_end\\\"] = idx\\n            else:\\n                if current_entity is not None:\\n                    entities.append(_finalize_entity(current_entity, tokens, char_offsets))\\n                current_entity = {\\\"type\\\": type_name, \\\"token_start\\\": idx, \\\"token_end\\\": idx}\\n\\n    if current_entity is not None:\\n        entities.append(_finalize_entity(current_entity, tokens, char_offsets))\\n\\n    return text, entities\\n\\n\\ndef _finalize_entity(entity: Dict, tokens: List[str], char_offsets: List[tuple]) -> Dict:\\n    start = char_offsets[entity[\\\"token_start\\\"]][0]\\n    end = char_offsets[entity[\\\"token_end\\\"]][1]\\n    span_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[entity[\\\"token_start\\\"]:entity[\\\"token_end\\\"] + 1])\\n    return {\\\"text\\\": span_text, \\\"type\\\": entity[\\\"type\\\"], \\\"start\\\": start, \\\"end\\\": end}\\n\\n\\ndef get_all_types(dataset: List[Dict]) -> List[str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract all unique fine-grained entity type names from dataset.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    types = set()\\n    for sample in dataset:\\n        for ent in sample[\\\"entities\\\"]:\\n            types.add(ent[\\\"type\\\"])\\n    return sorted(types)\\n\\n\\ndef load_type_definitions(path: str) -> Dict[str, str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load type name -> definition mapping from JSON file.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    with open(path, \\\"r\\\") as f:\\n        return json.load(f)\\n\\n\\ndef filter_entities_by_types(entities: List[Dict], type_set: Set[str]) -> List[Dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Filter entities to only keep those whose type is in type_set.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return [e for e in entities if e[\\\"type\\\"] in type_set]\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    import argparse\\n\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Few-NERD data loading utilities\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--split\\\", default=\\\"test\\\", help=\\\"Dataset split\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max_samples\\\", type=int, default=None, help=\\\"Max samples to load\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output_path\\\", default=None, help=\\\"Save processed data as JSON\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)\\n    data = load_few_nerd(split=args.split, max_samples=args.max_samples)\\n    types = get_all_types(data)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.41}}"}

entity[\\\"type\\\"], \\\"start\\\": start, \\\"end\\\": end}\\n\\n\\ndef get_all_types(dataset: List[Dict]) -> List[str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract all unique fine-grained entity type names from dataset.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    types = set()\\n    for sample in dataset:\\n        for ent in sample[\\\"entities\\\"]:\\n            types.add(ent[\\\"type\\\"])\\n    return sorted(types)\\n\\n\\ndef load_type_definitions(path: str) -> Dict[str, str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load type name -> definition mapping from JSON file.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    with open(path, \\\"r\\\") as f:\\n        return json.load(f)\\n\\n\\ndef filter_entities_by_types(entities: List[Dict], type_set: Set[str]) -> List[Dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Filter entities to only keep those whose type is in type_set.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return [e for e in entities if e[\\\"type\\\"] in type_set]\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    import argparse\\n\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Few-NERD data loading utilities\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--split\\\", default=\\\"test\\\", help=\\\"Dataset split\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max_samples\\\", type=int, default=None, help=\\\"Max samples to load\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output_path\\\", default=None, help=\\\"Save processed data as JSON\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)\\n    data = load_few_nerd(split=args.split, max_samples=args.max_samples)\\n    types = get_all_types(data)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.41}}"

_source line 135 · 2026-05-15T08:27:44.172Z_

No `.incomplete` files — download was interrupted, not active. Here's the full report:

---

## exp-012 兼容性报告

### 1. LLaMA 下载状态：**不完整**
- 已下载：13/15 safetensors 分片
- **缺失**：`model-00007-of-00015.safetensors`、`model-00015-of-00015.safetensors`
- config.json / tokenizer.json / tokenizer_config.json / hf_quant_config.json 均已就位
- 无活跃下载进程，需手动恢复

### 2. 代码兼容性：**完全兼容，无需改代码**

| 检查项 | 结果 |
|--------|------|
| model_path 传递 | `run_experiment.py` → `run_extraction()` → `vLLM LLM(model=...)` — 纯参数传递，无 hardcode |
| Qwen3-specific 逻辑 | 无。"Qwen3" 仅出现在注释和 help text 中，无条件分支 |
| vLLM 初始化 | `LLM(model=..., dtype="bfloat16", gpu_memory_utilization=0.95, enforce_eager=True, max_model_len=4096, trust_remote_code=True)` — 模型无关 |
| FP8 量化 | `hf_quant_config.json` 使用 nvidia modelopt FP8 格式，vLLM 0.20.2 自动检测，无需额外 `--quantization` 参数 |
| tensor_parallel | 代码默认 tp=1。FP8 70B ≈ 70GB，98GB GPU 可 fit（剩余 ~28GB 给 KV cache） |
| chat template | 代码用 `llm.generate(prompts)` 传原始文本，不走 chat template。LLaMA 和 Qwen3 行为一致 |
| prompt 格式 | 纯英文指令文本，无模型特定标记 |
| 输出解析 | `guided_json` 约束解码，模型无关 |
| compute_type_embeddings.py | 使用 `AutoModelForCausalLM` + `AutoTokenizer`，完全模型无关 |

### 3. Decision Point：BCS embeddings

根据 D010 发现，extraction model 的 embeddings 与 BCS-F1 相关性更强。两个选项：

- **Option A（快速）**：复用 Qwen3 embeddings (`artifacts/type_embeddings.pt` + `artifacts/sim_matrix.pt`)。BCS 值不反映 LLaMA 的类型混淆模式，但可以先跑 exp1 验证 pipeline，BCS correlation 分析后补。
- **Option B（严谨）**：先用 LLaMA 计算 Few-NERD embeddings。需要 GPU（当前被 exp-008 占用）。

**建议**：先用 Option A 跑 exp1+exp2，同时生成 LLaMA embeddings，之后再做 BCS reanalysis。exp1 的 count baseline 不依赖 BCS 分组，exp2 的 low/high BCS 分组虽基于 Qwen3 embeddings，但结果仍有 cross-model 对比价值。

### 4. 恢复下载命令

```bash
# 先恢复下载缺失分片
source /etc/network_turbo
export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache
export HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1
export REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt
export SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt

huggingface-cli download neuralmagic/Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct-FP8-dynamic \
  --local-dir /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8/ \
  --resume-download
```

### 5. Launch 命令草案

```bash
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh
conda activate base
cd /root/schema-dag-extraction

MODEL=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8/
COMMON="--dataset fewnerd --prompt_version v2 --types_file configs/few_nerd_types.json --embeddings_path artifacts/type_embeddings.pt --sim_matrix_path artifacts/sim_matrix.pt --model_path $MODEL --max_samples 1000 --max_model_len 4096 --output_dir artifacts/exp012"

# exp1: Count Baseline
echo "===== [$(date)] exp-012 exp1 ====="
python3 src/run_experiment.py --mode exp1 $COMMON

# exp2: Confusability Controlled
echo "===== [$(date)] exp-012 exp2 ====="
python3 src/run_experiment.py --mode exp2 $COMMON

echo "===== [$(date)] exp-012 done ====="
```

### 6. 需要 GPU 后执行（LLaMA embeddings）

```bash
python3 src/compute_type_embeddings.py \
